56	## Review
57	Run by the reviewer agent: the interface review after Phase 0, a phase review per package at each checkpoint, and the milestone exit. `dt land` already checked each landing's `Writes:`, protected paths and gates; review judges what tools can't.
58	
59	**Interface** (after Phase 0): the code is the contract, no more and no less.
60	1. Types match `contract.md`: fields, types, invariants. Nothing a step's In → Out needs is missing; no helper is frozen.
61	2. Signatures match the step tables, in their owner modules. Each stub names the open task that owns its step; no logic beyond types and validation.
62	3. One sample per case the contract names, valid and realistic, never holding a TC's expected value. Replay doubles replay an interaction the Protocol allows. Each fake passes its contract's cases; anything it does beyond echoing a fixture (filtering, ordering, an error) is a case the real provider must pass too. Contract tests check shape only, never a stub or other state a later task changes.
63	4. `__init__.py` exports exactly the contract; each `Mitigated by` in the plan's dependency graph exists; each split task's tester can write its tests from the frozen interface and its `Read:`.
64	
65	**Each phase**, per package, tests before code:
66	1. **Interfaces followed** (CR-09): no frozen type, signature or contract folder changed outside a Revise. Consumers use the declared types: no dict for a dataclass, no reaching into a provider's internals, no local redefinition. A stub is replaced only by the task it names; no cross-package import outside the contract; spine tests run on samples and doubles, not another step's code.
67	2. **Tests prove something:** each intent test asserts its TC case or acceptance bullet, loading expected values and tolerances from fixtures; none is assertion-free or weakened; Hypothesis runs the `ci` profile. Unit tests are specific, never computing the expected value with the code under test or testing only a mock. **Mutation check** (`.claude/agents/reviewer.md`): per acceptance bullet, the smallest change that should break it; a mutation no test catches is a finding.
68	3. **Correct to the spec:** each acceptance bullet truly met, not special-cased to the sample; the error paths the requirement names; seeding and determinism; numerical edge cases.
69	4. **Simple** (CR-11, CR-12, CR-42): no abstraction with one implementation outside the contract or an AD; no forwarding wrapper, one-method class, or class where a function does; no parameter, flag or config key every caller sets the same; no `try/except` that only re-raises or swallows, no checks the types already guarantee; no loop the library already provides. No near-copies within or across the phase's tasks: one concept is one function in its owner module (tests may repeat for readability). Each finding names the simpler form, with the same tests passing.
70	5. **Pipelines** (CR-10): entry functions read as their step tables (`dt layout`); step owners respected; spine baselines still selectable.
71	6. **The bar** (CR-07): diff this file, `pyproject.toml`, `scripts/` and `.claude/agents/` against the milestone's Base. Any loosening is Critical; new exceptions need an owner and expiry.
72	7. **Hidden and parallel risks:** where a real provider could act differently from its double; fixed output paths, global state, test-order dependence.
73	8. **The rest:** readability, security, performance where a QR sets a number; reused reference or probe code has its Caveats handled and fits the contract; `dt lint` clean, pre-build sections replaced by `dt layout` pointers.
74	
75	**Milestone exit**, over the whole diff, adds: the Scope's Exit holds; every Covers ID has a test; `check.sh full --exit` finds no stub; system tests use real providers; the same sample through a real provider and its double agrees; deferred requirements untouched; pip-audit ran.
76	
77	**Severity:** Critical: a frozen interface changed, a contract test pinning a transient state, a weakened test, an intent test that misses a mutation changing its TC's expected output, a wrong answer, a loosened bar, a security hole, lost data; it stops the build. Important: a consumer bypassing a type, any other surviving mutation, an unneeded abstraction, a duplicate across tasks. Suggestion and Nit: local. Findings go under the checkpoint block in `tasks/plan.md` (`dt finding`), each with a fix task or a reason.
78	
79	## Done
80	agent-skills `references/definition-of-done.md`, tailored once:
81	- **Correctness and Quality:** checked per task by the gate.
82	- **Integration and Documentation:** checked at each checkpoint.
83	- **The full list:** checked at milestone exit.

1	# rllab — Contract
2	
3	**Package:** rllab · **Status:** Approved · **Last updated:** 2026-10-19 · **Architecture:** [../../architecture.md](../../architecture.md)
4	
5	`Direction = Literal["higher", "lower"]`, `Settings = Mapping[str, int | float | str | bool]` and `Array = np.ndarray` are used throughout.
6	
7	## core
8	
9	### core.ActionSpace: type
10	`ActionSpace = Discrete(n: int) | Box(dims: int, low: float, high: float)`, both frozen dataclasses
11	- **Invariants:** `Discrete` actions are ints in `[0, n)`; `Box` actions are `dims` floats in `[low, high]`
12	- **Boundary:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.ActionSpace/ · **Cases:** snake `Discrete(3)`; fish `Box(2, -1.0, 1.0)`
13	- **Used by:** worlds, baselines, methods, refcheck
14	
15	### core.TeamSpec: type
16	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class TeamSpec: name: str; agents: tuple[int, ...]; score_name: str; direction: Direction`
17	- **Invariants:** `agents` are indices into the world's agent axis; teams of one world are disjoint and cover every agent
18	- **Boundary:** worlds, train, evaluate, charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.TeamSpec/ · **Cases:** snake's one team; predator-prey's prey (6) and predator (2) teams
19	- **Used by:** every component reading scores
20	
21	### core.WorldSpec: type
22	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class WorldSpec: world: str; variant: str; agents: tuple[str, ...]; teams: tuple[TeamSpec, ...]; obs_len: int; action: ActionSpace; max_steps: int; measures: tuple[MeasureSpec, ...]; sight: float | None`
23	`MeasureSpec(name: str, team: str, direction: Direction)`
24	- **Invariants:** available without running an episode (WORLD-04); `sight` is the radius in world units for local-sight variants, else `None`; equality of two specs is field by field
25	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, runlog, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.WorldSpec/ · **Cases:** snake default; fish local-sight (8 agents, sight set); predator-prey
26	- **Used by:** all components; PL-02, PL-08
27	
28	### core.Step: type
29	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Step: obs: Array; rewards: Array; active: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool`
30	- **Invariants:** `obs` float32 `n_agents × obs_len`; `rewards` float32 `n_agents`; `active` bool `n_agents`, and an inactive agent's row is zeros with reward 0 (WORLD edge case); `truncated` is true only when `max_steps` ended the episode
31	- **Boundary:** worlds, methods, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Step/ · **Cases:** a snake mid-episode step; a predator-prey step with one prey caught
32	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, refcheck
33	
34	### core.EpisodeResult: type
35	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EpisodeResult: seed: int; length: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; team_rewards: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
36	- **Invariants:** `scores` and `team_rewards` keyed by team name (WORLD-05, LOG-02); `measures` keyed by every `MeasureSpec.name`
37	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EpisodeResult/ · **Cases:** a snake episode with 12 food; a predator-prey episode with both team scores
38	- **Used by:** train, runlog, evaluate, refcheck
39	
40	### core.DrawState: type
41	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class DrawState: width: float; height: float; objects: tuple[Drawable, ...]`
42	`Drawable(kind: str, x: float, y: float, size: float, team: str | None, agent: int | None)`
43	- **Invariants:** lists every object on screen at that step (WORLD-06); snake cells use grid coordinates with `size` 1; `agent` set for agents, so the viewer can draw `WorldSpec.sight` around them
44	- **Boundary:** worlds, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.DrawState/ · **Cases:** a snake board; an 8-fish tank with the ball
45	- **Used by:** replay
46	
47	### core.Frame: type
48	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Frame: step: int; draw: DrawState; scores: Mapping[str, float]; rewards: Array`
49	- **Invariants:** `scores` is each team's running score after this step; `rewards` the latest per agent (REPLAY-05); frame 0 is the reset state with zero rewards
50	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Frame/ · **Cases:** frame 0 and frame 1 of seed 7 snake
51	- **Used by:** replay
52	
53	### core.Episode: type
54	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Episode: result: EpisodeResult; frames: tuple[Frame, ...] | None; hit_step_limit: bool`
55	- **Invariants:** `frames` is set only when traced, with `result.length + 1` entries
56	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Episode/ · **Cases:** an untraced snake episode; a traced 3-step check-world episode
57	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
58	
59	### core.World: type
60	```python
61	class World(Protocol):
62	    spec: WorldSpec
63	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step: ...
64	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step: ...
65	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult: ...
66	    def draw(self) -> DrawState: ...
67	```
68	- **Invariants:** same seed → same start (WORLD-01); same seed and actions → same steps (WORLD-02); `actions` has one row per agent, rows of inactive agents ignored
69	- **Errors:** `step` raises `ActionError(agent: str, value: object)`, a `ValueError`, for an action outside `spec.action`, never clipping it; `step` after `done` raises `RuntimeError`
70	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.World/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a recorded 5-step snake episode from seed 42
71	- **Used by:** every component that plays episodes; PL-02, PL-08
72	
73	### core.Policy: type
74	`class Policy(Protocol): def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array`
75	- **Invariants:** `obs` is `k × obs_len` for the `k` agents of one team; returns `k` actions inside the world's `ActionSpace`; deterministic for a given `obs` and `rng` state
76	- **Boundary:** baselines, methods, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Policy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule policy over 5 observations
77	- **Used by:** core.play_episode, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-08
78	
79	### core.play_episode: The one episode loop
80	`def play_episode(world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False) -> Episode`
81	- **Invariants:** `policies` keyed by team; the seed spawns the world's and each policy's generator (AD-05), so the same inputs give the same `Episode`; stops at `done` or `spec.max_steps`
82	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a team with no policy; `ActionError` propagates from `World.step`
83	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.play_episode/ · **Cases:** snake rule policy, seed 7, traced
84	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck; PL-02, PL-08
85	
86	### core.EVAL_SEEDS: In-training evaluation seeds
87	`EVAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(1_000_000, 1_000_020))`
88	- **Invariants:** 20 seeds, disjoint from training seeds `[0, 10⁶)` and from `FINAL_SEEDS` (TRAIN-04)
89	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.EVAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 20 seeds as listed
90	- **Used by:** train, evaluate; PL-02, PL-05, PL-12
91	
92	### core.FINAL_SEEDS: Final evaluation seeds
93	`FINAL_SEEDS: tuple[int, ...] = tuple(range(2_000_000, 2_000_100))`
94	- **Invariants:** 100 seeds, shared by every run, baseline and reference (EVAL-03, CHECK-02)
95	- **Boundary:** evaluate, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.FINAL_SEEDS/ · **Cases:** the 100 seeds as listed
96	- **Used by:** evaluate, refcheck; PL-06, PL-09
97	
98	### core.Output: type
99	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Output: path: Path; kind: Literal["chart", "video", "writeup"]; made_from: tuple[str, ...]`
100	- **Invariants:** `path` exists when returned; `made_from` names the runs, checkpoints and seeds behind the file, so a write-up can cite it (NOTES-02)
101	- **Boundary:** charts, replay, notes, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.Output/ · **Cases:** a 5-run snake learning-curve PNG; a seed-7 snake video
102	- **Used by:** charts, replay, notes, cli; PL-05, PL-08, PL-10, PL-12
103	
104	### core.MethodConfig: type
105	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class MethodConfig: method: Literal["dqn", "ppo"]; settings: Settings`
106	- **Invariants:** `settings` holds only keys the method declares, checked by `train.load_run_configs`; missing keys take the method's defaults, written back in full to the run record; owned by `core` so `runlog` can store it (AD-12)
107	- **Boundary:** methods, train, runlog, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.MethodConfig/ · **Cases:** snake DQN defaults; PPO with one overridden learning rate
108	- **Used by:** methods, train, runlog, refcheck; PL-01, PL-03
109	
110	### core.RunConfig: type
111	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunConfig: name: str; world: str; variant: str; methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]; seed: int; budget: int; check_settings: Mapping[str, Settings]; device: str = "cpu"`
112	- **Invariants:** `methods` has one entry per team (TRAIN-07); `budget` counts world steps, not agent steps; `check_settings` overrides settings for the check world only (AD-09); owned by `core` so `runlog` can store it (AD-12)
113	- **Boundary:** train, runlog, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.RunConfig/ · **Cases:** a snake config from `configs/snake-default.toml`; a predator-prey config with two methods
114	- **Used by:** train, runlog, refcheck, cli; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03, PL-09
115	
116	### core.BaselineMeans: type
117	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class BaselineMeans: world: str; variant: str; random: Mapping[str, float]; rule: Mapping[str, float]`
118	- **Invariants:** `random` and `rule` keyed by team; means over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes, the seeds the in-training curve uses; in predator-prey each team's baselines play the rule-based opponent; produced by `baselines.baseline_means`, owned by `core` so `charts` can take it (AD-12)
119	- **Boundary:** baselines, charts, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/core.BaselineMeans/ · **Cases:** snake; predator-prey with both teams
120	- **Used by:** baselines, charts, cli; PL-05
121	
122	## worlds
123	
124	### worlds.make: World factory
125	`def make(world: str, variant: str = "default", **overrides: int | float) -> World`
126	- **Invariants:** worlds `snake` (`default`), `fish` (`one`, `many`, `local`), `prey` (`default`), `check-discrete`, `check-continuous`; `overrides` change only the spec's starting values (grid size, counts, step limits; spec Assumptions)
127	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming an unknown world, variant or override
128	- **Boundary:** baselines, train, evaluate, replay, refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/worlds.make/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded seed-42 snake double for `make("snake")`
129	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay, refcheck, baselines; PL-02, PL-08
130	
131	### worlds.CheckWorld: type
132	`class CheckWorld(World, Protocol): def states(self) -> Array; def best_actions(self) -> Array`
133	- **Invariants:** `states()` is the observation of every check-world state, `n × obs_len`; `best_actions()` the hand-derived best action for each, to within 0.1 per dimension for `Box` actions
134	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/worlds.CheckWorld/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the 6-state two-exit corridor; one of the 1-D target point
135	- **Used by:** train; PL-01
136	
137	## baselines
138	
139	### baselines.RandomPolicy: Uniform random play
140	`class RandomPolicy(Policy): def __init__(self, action: ActionSpace) -> None`
141	- **Invariants:** each action drawn uniformly from the declared action format (EVAL-01), using only the `rng` passed to `act`
142	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RandomPolicy/ · **Cases:** 5 snake actions from a fixed `rng`; 5 fish pushes from a fixed `rng`
143	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay; PL-05
144	
145	### baselines.RulePolicy: type
146	`class RulePolicy(Policy, Protocol): description: str`
147	- **Invariants:** acts on observations only; `description` is the world's one sentence (EVAL-02)
148	- **Boundary:** evaluate, replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.RulePolicy/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the snake rule over 5 observations; the fish rule seeing no ball
149	- **Used by:** evaluate, replay, cli; PL-05
150	
151	### baselines.rule_for: Rule baseline lookup
152	`def rule_for(world: str, variant: str, team: str) -> RulePolicy`
153	- **Invariants:** SNAKE-07, FISH-09 and PREY-07 rules; deterministic except FISH-09's and PREY-07's random fallbacks, which use the `rng` passed to `act`
154	- **Errors:** `KeyError` naming a world, variant or team with no rule
155	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.rule_for/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded snake rule double
156	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, replay
157	
158	### baselines.baseline_means: Baseline score lines
159	`def baseline_means(world: str, variant: str) -> BaselineMeans`
160	- **Invariants:** plays `RandomPolicy` and `rule_for` per team through `core.play_episode`; deterministic
161	- **Errors:** `KeyError` from `rule_for`
162	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/baselines.baseline_means/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake `BaselineMeans`
163	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
164	
165	## methods
166	
167	### methods.Transition: type
168	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Transition: obs: Array; actions: Array; rewards: Array; next_obs: Array; done: bool; truncated: bool; active: Array`
169	- **Invariants:** rows are the team's agents in `TeamSpec.agents` order; `active` masks agents that had already left before this step
170	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Transition/ · **Cases:** a snake transition; a many-fish transition of 8 agents
171	- **Used by:** train
172	
173	### methods.Learner: type
174	```python
175	class Learner(Protocol):
176	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...   # exploring
177	    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...                       # updates on its own schedule
178	    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...                                      # frozen, deterministic copy
179	    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...
180	```
181	- **Invariants:** hand-written learning code only (CHECK-01); `policy()` is greedy (DQN) or the mean action (PPO) and does not change as the learner trains
182	- **Errors:** `observe` raises `NonFiniteError(value: float)` when a loss or parameter is NaN or infinite, before changing any parameter
183	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.Learner/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a DQN learner over 10 snake transitions
184	- **Used by:** train; PL-01, PL-02, PL-03
185	
186	### methods.make_learner: Learner factory
187	`def make_learner(config: MethodConfig, spec: WorldSpec, team: str, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Learner`
188	- **Invariants:** `dqn` needs a `Discrete` action space and `ppo` a `Box` one
189	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming the method and the action format on a mismatch
190	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.make_learner/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the recorded DQN learner double
191	- **Used by:** train; PL-02
192	
193	### methods.load_policy: Checkpoint to policy
194	`def load_policy(ckpt: Checkpoint, team: str, spec: WorldSpec) -> Policy`
195	- **Invariants:** the same checkpoint and team give a policy that acts identically every time
196	- **Errors:** `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing field (observation length, action format) when `ckpt.spec` doesn't match `spec` (REPLAY edge case)
197	- **Boundary:** evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/methods.load_policy/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake rule policy for any snake checkpoint
198	- **Used by:** evaluate, replay; PL-08
199	
200	## train
201	
202	### train.load_run_configs: Config loader
203	`def load_run_configs(path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int]) -> list[RunConfig]`
204	- **Invariants:** one config per seed, named `<file stem>-s<seed>`; method settings missing from the TOML take the method's defaults
205	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` for a missing file; `ValueError` naming an unknown key, world, variant or method
206	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.load_run_configs/ · **Cases:** a fake returning snake configs for seeds 1 and 2
207	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-01, PL-03, PL-09
208	
209	### train.run_check: Method check
210	`def run_check(config_path: Path, seed: int = 0) -> list[CheckResult]`
211	- **Invariants:** one result per distinct team method in the config, trained on its check world (`check-discrete` for `dqn`, `check-continuous` for `ppo`) with `check_settings`, for the fixed check budget; under a minute on the laptop (TRAIN-06, AD-09)
212	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing DQN check
213	- **Used by:** cli; PL-01
214	
215	### train.run_train: One training run
216	`def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord`
217	- **Invariants:** exactly `budget` world steps (TRAIN-01); checkpoints at 0%, 10%, … 100% (TRAIN-03); evaluation of every team on `EVAL_SEEDS` at every 5% (TRAIN-04); same config on the same machine → identical logs and checkpoints (TRAIN-02)
218	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` before any work if the run folder exists; `NonFiniteError` ends the run as `failed` with step and value, keeping the last good checkpoint; interruption marks it `incomplete`
219	- **Boundary:** PL-03 · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_train/ · **Cases:** a fake writing the finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
220	- **Used by:** train.run_batch; PL-02, PL-03
221	
222	### train.run_batch: Training batch
223	`def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]`
224	- **Invariants:** loads one config per seed, runs the method check with `check_seed` before any run, then one process per config up to `workers` (default: the core count), each with one torch thread (TRAIN-05, TRAIN-06, AD-05)
225	- **Errors:** `CheckFailedError(result: CheckResult)` before any run starts; a failing run doesn't stop the others
226	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.run_batch/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 2 finished snake records
227	- **Used by:** cli; PL-03
228	
229	### train.CheckResult: type
230	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CheckResult: method: str; world: str; agreement: float; passed: bool`
231	- **Invariants:** `agreement` is the share of check-world states where the trained policy picks the best action; `passed` is `agreement >= 0.95` (TRAIN-06)
232	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/train.CheckResult/ · **Cases:** a passing DQN check; a failing PPO check at 0.80
233	- **Used by:** cli; PL-01, PL-03
234	
235	## runlog
236	
237	### runlog.RunRecord: type
238	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class RunRecord: config: RunConfig; spec: WorldSpec; started: datetime; ended: datetime | None; status: Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]; failure: Failure | None; path: Path`
239	`Failure(step: int, value: float, checkpoint: Path)`
240	- **Invariants:** every LOG-01 field present once the run ends; `config` is a `core.RunConfig` (AD-12), so `runlog` imports only `core`; `config.methods` holds full settings, defaults included
241	- **Boundary:** train, evaluate, charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.RunRecord/ · **Cases:** a finished snake run; a failed run with a NaN at step 41,200; an incomplete run
242	- **Used by:** train, evaluate, charts, refcheck, cli; PL-02, PL-03
243	
244	### runlog.EvalPoint: type
245	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class EvalPoint: step: int; scores: Mapping[str, float]; measures: Mapping[str, float]`
246	- **Invariants:** means over the 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes, per team and per measure; a finished run has 21, at 0%, 5%, … 100% (TRAIN-04)
247	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.EvalPoint/ · **Cases:** snake point at 50%; predator-prey point with both teams
248	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, cli
249	
250	### runlog.Checkpoint: type
251	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Checkpoint: run: str; pct: int; step: int; spec: WorldSpec; methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]; state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]; path: Path`
252	- **Invariants:** `pct` in {0, 10, …, 100}; `methods` holds `core.MethodConfig` values (AD-12); `state` keyed by team; saved and loaded through `torch.save`/`torch.load(weights_only=True)`
253	- **Boundary:** methods, evaluate, replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Checkpoint/ · **Cases:** the 0% and 100% checkpoints of a short snake run
254	- **Used by:** methods, evaluate, replay; PL-07, PL-08
255	
256	### runlog.Run: type
257	```python
258	class Run(Protocol):
259	    record: RunRecord
260	    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...
261	    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...
262	    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...
263	```
264	- **Invariants:** read-only view of one run folder; readable while the run writes (LOG-06)
265	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, replay, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Run/ · **Cases:** a replay double of a finished 3-checkpoint snake run folder
266	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, replay, refcheck, cli; PL-05, PL-06, PL-12
267	
268	### runlog.RunWriter: Run folder writer
269	```python
270	class RunWriter:
271	    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None: ...
272	    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None: ...
273	    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None: ...      # flushes every log
274	    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint: ...
275	    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord: ...
276	```
277	- **Errors:** `RunExistsError` naming the path if `runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>` exists (TRAIN edge case)
278	- **Boundary:** train · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.RunWriter/ · **Cases:** a replay double of the writes of a 3-checkpoint snake run
279	- **Used by:** train; PL-02
280	
281	### runlog.find_runs: Run lookup
282	`def find_runs(root: Path, world: str, variant: str, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()) -> list[Run]`
283	- **Invariants:** finished runs only, plus incomplete or failed runs named in `include` (TRAIN edge case); a `running` run with no live process is reported as incomplete
284	- **Boundary:** charts, evaluate, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.find_runs/ · **Cases:** a fake returning 5 finished snake runs plus one named incomplete run
285	- **Used by:** charts, evaluate, refcheck, cli; PL-05, PL-06
286	
287	### runlog.open_run: Open one run
288	`def open_run(path: Path) -> Run`
289	- **Invariants:** any status, including `running`, `incomplete` and `failed`
290	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if no `run.json` is at `path`
291	- **Boundary:** replay, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.open_run/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the finished 3-checkpoint snake run double
292	- **Used by:** replay, cli; PL-12
293	
294	### runlog.Progress: type
295	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Progress: step: int; budget: int; latest: EvalPoint | None; elapsed: timedelta; status: str`
296	- **Invariants:** at most one evaluation point behind the run (LOG-06)
297	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.Progress/ · **Cases:** a run at 35% with the 35% point written
298	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04
299	
300	### runlog.progress: Live run view
301	`def progress(path: Path) -> Progress`
302	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` if no run is at `path`
303	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/runlog.progress/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 35% progress
304	- **Used by:** cli; PL-04
305	
306	## charts
307	
308	### charts.learning_curve: Learning-curve chart
309	`def learning_curve(runs: Sequence[Run], baselines: BaselineMeans, out: Path, *, measure: str | None = None) -> Output`
310	- **Invariants:** draws per team a mean line, a min–max band and both baseline lines against "environment steps" (LOG-03, PREY-10), or a declared measure the same way (LOG-05); follows the `dataviz` skill (LOG-04); under 3 runs, titled with the count and a band note
311	- **Errors:** `MixedRunsError` naming the differing settings when runs differ by more than seed
312	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/charts.learning_curve/ · **Cases:** a fake writing a placeholder PNG for 5 snake runs
313	- **Used by:** cli; PL-05
314	
315	### charts.pairing_heatmap: Pairing-grid chart
316	`def pairing_heatmap(grid: PairingGrid, out: Path) -> Output`
317	- **Invariants:** one 11 × 11 heatmap per team, prey checkpoints on one axis and predator checkpoints on the other; follows the `dataviz` skill
318	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/charts.pairing_heatmap/ · **Cases:** a fake writing a placeholder PNG for the planted grid
319	- **Used by:** cli; PL-12
320	
321	## evaluate
322	
323	### evaluate.Comparison: type
324	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Comparison: baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]; team: str; learned_mean: float; baseline_mean: float; difference: float; p_raw: float; p_holm: float; rejected: bool`
325	- **Invariants:** `difference` is signed so positive means better in the team's direction
326	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Comparison/ · **Cases:** a rejected snake-vs-random; a kept snake-vs-rule
327	- **Used by:** evaluate.Summary
328	
329	### evaluate.compare: Paired permutation test
330	`def compare(learned: Array, baseline: Array, direction: Direction, *, permutations: int = 10_000, rng: np.random.Generator) -> tuple[float, float]`
331	- **Invariants:** `learned` is runs × seeds, averaged over runs per seed; `baseline` has one score per seed, on the same seeds; swaps labels within each seed; returns (difference, one-sided p) as EVAL-04 states; built and checked with the `null-hypothesis-testing` skill
332	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if seed counts differ
333	- **Used by:** evaluate.final_evaluation
334	
335	### evaluate.Summary: type
336	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Summary: world: str; variant: str; team: str; runs: tuple[str, ...]; learned_mean: float; learned_range: tuple[float, float]; run_means: Mapping[str, float]; random_mean: float; rule_mean: float; comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]; verdict: Verdict; collapse: int | None`
337	`Verdict(beats_baselines: bool, failing_runs: tuple[str, ...], runs_needed: int)`
338	- **Invariants:** one per world, variant and team (EVAL-06, PREY-08); `comparisons` empty and `runs_needed > 0` with fewer than 5 runs; `collapse` is set when the team's in-training eval score stays at or below its random-baseline mean for the last 4+ consecutive eval points, and is the step of the first of them
339	- **Boundary:** charts, refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.Summary/ · **Cases:** a passing snake summary; one with a failing run; one with 3 runs
340	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
341	
342	### evaluate.final_evaluation: Final evaluation
343	`def final_evaluation(runs: Sequence[Run], out: Path, *, workers: int | None = None) -> list[Summary]`
344	- **Invariants:** plays every final checkpoint and both baselines on `FINAL_SEEDS` (EVAL-03); Holm across the 2 comparisons per team; a pass needs both nulls rejected (EVAL-04) and every run's mean beating the rule mean (EVAL-05); in predator-prey each team plays against the rule-based opponent (PREY-08); within 10 minutes (QR-05)
345	- **Boundary:** refcheck, cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.final_evaluation/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
346	- **Used by:** refcheck, cli; PL-06
347	
348	### evaluate.load_summaries: Saved summaries
349	`def load_summaries(root: Path, world: str, variant: str) -> list[Summary]`
350	- **Invariants:** the latest `_eval/<date>/summary.json` for that world and variant, one `Summary` per team
351	- **Errors:** `FileNotFoundError` naming `rllab evaluate` when none exists
352	- **Boundary:** refcheck · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.load_summaries/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the passing snake summary
353	- **Used by:** refcheck; PL-09
354	
355	### evaluate.PairingGrid: type
356	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class PairingGrid: run: str; prey_pcts: tuple[int, ...]; predator_pcts: tuple[int, ...]; scores: Mapping[str, Array]`
357	- **Invariants:** `scores` keyed by team, each 11 × 11 (one row and column per checkpoint), cell [i, j] the mean of 20 `EVAL_SEEDS` episodes of prey checkpoint i against predator checkpoint j (PREY-09)
358	- **Boundary:** charts · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.PairingGrid/ · **Cases:** an 11 × 11 grid with planted values
359	- **Used by:** charts; PL-12
360	
361	### evaluate.pairing_grid: Checkpoint pairings
362	`def pairing_grid(run: Run, *, workers: int | None = None) -> PairingGrid`
363	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if the run's world doesn't have exactly two teams
364	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/evaluate.pairing_grid/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the planted 11 × 11 grid
365	- **Used by:** cli; PL-12
366	
367	## replay
368	
369	### replay.Trace: type
370	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Trace: label: str; spec: WorldSpec; seed: int; frames: tuple[Frame, ...]; hit_step_limit: bool`
371	- **Invariants:** same sources and seed → equal frames (REPLAY-02); `label` names the checkpoint or baseline per team
372	- **Boundary:** replay · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Trace/ · **Cases:** the rule snake on seed 7
373	- **Used by:** replay.view, replay.save_video; PL-07, PL-08
374	
375	### replay.open_sides: Replay sides
376	`def open_sides(world: str, variant: str, sides: Sequence[str]) -> list[Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]]`
377	- **Invariants:** one or two sides, each `<run-path>@<pct>`, `random` or `rule`; predator-prey sides may name each team (`prey=<run>@<pct>,predator=<run>@<pct>`); checkpoints read through `runlog.open_run`
378	- **Errors:** `ValueError` naming a malformed side or a third side; `SpecMismatchError` naming each differing field when a checkpoint's spec no longer matches the world (REPLAY edge case)
379	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_sides/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the `rule` side and a 100% snake checkpoint side
380	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
381	
382	### replay.open_sources: One replay side
383	`def open_sources(world: str, variant: str, side: str) -> Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]`
384	- **Invariants:** one side in `open_sides`'s format
385	- **Errors:** as `open_sides`
386	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.open_sources/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a 50% snake checkpoint side
387	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
388	
389	### replay.policies_for: Side to policies
390	`def policies_for(sources: Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]], spec: WorldSpec) -> Mapping[str, Policy]`
391	- **Invariants:** one policy per team of `spec`; a team left out takes its policy from the first checkpoint given, so one predator-prey checkpoint replays both teams (REPLAY-01, REPLAY-06, PREY-09)
392	- **Errors:** `SpecMismatchError` from `methods.load_policy`
393	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.policies_for/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the snake rule policy
394	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
395	
396	### replay.to_trace: Episode to trace
397	`def to_trace(episode: Episode, spec: WorldSpec, label: str) -> Trace`
398	- **Errors:** `ValueError` if `episode.frames` is `None`
399	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.to_trace/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rule-snake seed-7 trace
400	- **Used by:** cli, replay.trace_sides; PL-08
401	
402	### replay.trace_sides: Traces for each side
403	`def trace_sides(world: str, variant: str, sides: Sequence[Mapping[str, Checkpoint | Literal["random", "rule"]]], seed: int) -> list[Trace]`
404	- **Invariants:** per side, a fresh world, `policies_for`, `core.play_episode` (traced) and `to_trace`; every side starts from the same seed (REPLAY-02, REPLAY-04)
405	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.trace_sides/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the rule snake and random snake traces on seed 7
406	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
407	
408	### replay.Session: type
409	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class Session: labels: tuple[str, ...]; last_step: tuple[int, ...]; speed: float; overlay_sight: bool`
410	- **Invariants:** one entry per trace shown; `last_step` is the frame each trace showed when the window closed; `speed` in {0.25, 1, 4, 16}
411	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.Session/ · **Cases:** one trace closed at step 30; two traces with one ended early
412	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
413	
414	### replay.view: Replay window
415	`def view(traces: Sequence[Trace], *, overlay_sight: bool = False, keys: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> Session`
416	- **Invariants:** one or two traces side by side, stepping in sync, a finished trace holding its last frame (REPLAY-04); pause, step ±1 and 0.25×/1×/4×/16× where 1× is 30 steps/s (REPLAY-03, QR-04); step, scores and rewards shown (REPLAY-05); a sight toggle when `spec.sight` is set (REPLAY-08); "stopped at step limit" shown when `hit_step_limit`; with `keys`, plays those key presses in a hidden window and closes, so it runs headless in tests
417	- **Errors:** `ValueError` for more than two traces
418	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.view/ · **Cases:** a fake returning a `Session` for the traces and overlay flag it was given
419	- **Used by:** cli; PL-07
420	
421	### replay.save_video: Replay to MP4
422	`def save_video(trace: Trace, out: Path, *, overlay_sight: bool = False) -> Output`
423	- **Invariants:** the whole episode at 1× (30 frames/s) as H.264 MP4 that plays in a standard player (REPLAY-07); offline (QR-06)
424	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/replay.save_video/ · **Cases:** a fake writing an empty file at `out`
425	- **Used by:** cli; PL-08
426	
427	## refcheck
428	
429	### refcheck.CheckReport: type
430	`@dataclass(frozen=True) class CheckReport: world: str; variant: str; team: str; reference_mean: float; reference_range: tuple[float, float]; hand_mean: float; hand_range: tuple[float, float]; ratio: float; outcome: Literal["pass", "investigate-low", "investigate-high"]`
431	- **Invariants:** `ratio` is hand ÷ reference; for higher-is-better a pass is 0.8 ≤ ratio ≤ 1.5, and for lower-is-better 2/3 ≤ ratio ≤ 1.25 (CHECK-03, CHECK edge case)
432	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/refcheck.CheckReport/ · **Cases:** 18 vs 20 food (pass); 15 vs 20 (investigate-low)
433	- **Used by:** cli; PL-09
434	
435	### refcheck.reference_check: Reference implementation check
436	`def reference_check(configs: Sequence[RunConfig], root: Path) -> list[CheckReport]`
437	- **Invariants:** trains SB3 `DQN` or `PPO` for each config (same world, budget and seeds as the hand-written runs; AD-08) under `_reference/`, evaluates on `FINAL_SEEDS` (CHECK-02), and compares with `evaluate.load_summaries`; one report per team; the only code importing `stable_baselines3` or `gymnasium`
438	- **Errors:** `ImportError` naming the `check` extra when it isn't installed; `FileNotFoundError` from `load_summaries` when the hand-written runs have no final evaluation
439	- **Boundary:** cli · **Fixtures:** tests/contracts/refcheck.reference_check/ · **Cases:** a fake returning the 18-vs-20 passing report
440	- **Used by:** cli; PL-09
441

463	## cli
464	
465	### cli.main: The rllab command
466	`def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int`
467	- **Invariants:** subcommands `check`, `train`, `batch`, `watch`, `chart`, `evaluate`, `grid`, `refcheck`, `replay`, `video`, `notes new`, `notes check`, each dispatched to its pipeline entry (`train` is a one-seed `batch`); prints the entry's returned contract type; owns no pipeline step (AD-10); every error is printed as one line naming its cause, exit code 1

+from rllab.core import Array, Policy
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Transition:
+    """One world step for one team; rows follow `TeamSpec.agents` order."""
+
+    obs: Array
+    actions: Array
+    rewards: Array
+    next_obs: Array
+    done: bool
+    truncated: bool
+    active: Array
+
+
+class Learner(Protocol):
+    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array: ...
+
+    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None: ...
+
+    def policy(self) -> Policy: ...
+
+    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]: ...
+
+
+class NonFiniteError(ArithmeticError):
+    """A loss or parameter became NaN or infinite; raised before any parameter changes."""
+
+    def __init__(self, value: float) -> None:
+        super().__init__(f"non-finite value {value}")
+        self.value = value
+
+
+class SpecMismatchError(ValueError):
+    """A checkpoint's world spec differs from the world it is loaded for."""
+
+    def __init__(self, fields: Sequence[str]) -> None:
+        super().__init__(f"checkpoint spec differs in: {', '.join(fields)}")
+        self.fields = tuple(fields)

64	```python
65	def run_batch(config_path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int], root: Path, *, workers: int | None = None, check_seed: int = 0) -> list[RunRecord]:
66	    configs = load_run_configs(config_path, seeds)
67	    plan = check_plan(configs)
68	    learners = train_checks(plan, check_seed)
69	    results = score_checks(learners, plan)
70	    require_pass(results)
71	    return run_parallel(configs, root, workers)
72	```

154	| 4 | `play_episode` | core.episode | World, Policy per team, seed → Episode | reset, act per team, step until done or `max_steps`, frames kept | REPLAY-02, WORLD-02 |
155	| 5 | `to_trace` | replay.tracing | Episode, WorldSpec, label → Trace | copy frames, set label and step-limit flag | REPLAY-02 |

159	```python
160	def run_video(world: str, variant: str, side: str, seed: int, out: Path, *, overlay: bool = False) -> Output:
161	    sources = open_sources(world, variant, side)
162	    w = make(world, variant)
163	    policies = policies_for(sources, w.spec)
164	    episode = play_episode(w, policies, seed, trace=True)
165	    trace = to_trace(episode, w.spec, side)
166	    return save_video(trace, out, overlay_sight=overlay)
167	```

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..2f133e4
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/rllab/runlog/types.py
@@ -0,0 +1,76 @@
+"""Types of run folders: record, evaluation points, checkpoints, the run view and progress."""
+
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from datetime import datetime, timedelta
+from pathlib import Path
+from typing import Literal, Protocol
+
+from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, MethodConfig, RunConfig, WorldSpec
+
+RunStatus = Literal["running", "finished", "incomplete", "failed"]
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Failure:
+    step: int
+    value: float
+    checkpoint: Path
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class RunRecord:
+    config: RunConfig
+    spec: WorldSpec
+    started: datetime
+    ended: datetime | None
+    status: RunStatus
+    failure: Failure | None
+    path: Path
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class EvalPoint:
+    step: int
+    scores: Mapping[str, float]
+    measures: Mapping[str, float]
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Checkpoint:
+    run: str
+    pct: int
+    step: int
+    spec: WorldSpec
+    methods: Mapping[str, MethodConfig]
+    state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]
+    path: Path
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Progress:
+    step: int
+    budget: int
+    latest: EvalPoint | None
+    elapsed: timedelta
+    status: str
+
+
+class Run(Protocol):
+    """A read-only view of one run folder, readable while the run writes (LOG-06)."""
+
+    record: RunRecord
+
+    def episodes(self) -> list[EpisodeResult]: ...
+
+    def evals(self) -> list[EvalPoint]: ...
+
+    def checkpoints(self) -> list[Checkpoint]: ...
+
+
+class RunExistsError(FileExistsError):
+    """The run folder already exists; nothing was written."""
+
+    def __init__(self, path: Path) -> None:
+        super().__init__(f"run folder {path} already exists")
+        self.path = path

+"""Writes one run folder: record, logs and checkpoints (AD-06)."""
+
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from rllab.core import EpisodeResult, RunConfig, WorldSpec
+from rllab.runlog.types import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunRecord
+
+
+class RunWriter:
+    """Creates `runs/<world>/<variant>/<name>` under `root`; refuses an existing one."""
+
+    def __init__(self, root: Path, config: RunConfig, spec: WorldSpec) -> None:
+        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")
+
+    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
+        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")
+
+    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None:
+        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")
+
+    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint:
+        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")
+
+    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord:
+        raise NotImplementedError("M1-T09")

new file mode 100644
index 0000000..43b356d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/rllab/train/types.py
@@ -0,0 +1,23 @@
+"""Types of training: the method check's result and its refusal."""
+
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class CheckResult:
+    """One method's check-world result; `passed` is `agreement >= 0.95` (TRAIN-06)."""
+
+    method: str
+    world: str
+    agreement: float
+    passed: bool
+
+
+class CheckFailedError(RuntimeError):
+    """A method failed its check world, so the batch starts no run."""
+
+    def __init__(self, result: CheckResult) -> None:
+        super().__init__(
+            f"method {result.method} failed {result.world}: agreement {result.agreement:.2f}"
+        )
+        self.result = result

+"""Types of the charts."""
+

+
+

+    """The runs differ by more than their seed, so one chart can't summarise them."""
+

diff --git a/src/rllab/cli/command.py b/src/rllab/cli/command.py
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..343691d
--- /dev/null
+++ b/src/rllab/cli/command.py
@@ -0,0 +1,107 @@
+"""The `rllab` command: parses a trigger, calls its pipeline entry and prints the result (AD-10)."""
+
+import argparse
+import sys
+from collections.abc import Sequence
+from pathlib import Path
+
+from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl04_watch import run_watch
+from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl05_chart import run_chart
+from rllab.cli.pipelines.pl06_evaluate import run_evaluate
+from rllab.train import run_batch, run_check
+
+
+def parse_seeds(text: str) -> list[int]:
+    """`3`, `1-5` or `1,3,5` to a list of seeds."""
+    seeds: list[int] = []
+    for part in text.split(","):
+        first, _, last = part.partition("-")
+        seeds.extend(range(int(first), int(last or first) + 1))
+    return seeds
+
+
+def _check(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
+    return run_check(args.config, args.seed)
+
+
+def _train(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
+    return run_batch(args.config, [args.seed], args.root, check_seed=args.check_seed)
+
+
+def _batch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
+    return run_batch(
+        args.config, args.seeds, args.root, workers=args.workers, check_seed=args.check_seed
+    )
+
+
+def _watch(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
+    return run_watch(args.run_path)
+
+
+def _chart(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
+    return run_chart(
+        args.root, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, measure=args.measure
+    )
+
+
+def _evaluate(args: argparse.Namespace) -> object:
+    return run_evaluate(
+        args.root, args.world, args.variant, include=args.include, workers=args.workers
+    )
+
+
+def _parser() -> argparse.ArgumentParser:
+    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(prog="rllab")
+    sub = parser.add_subparsers(dest="command", required=True)
+    root = argparse.ArgumentParser(add_help=False)
+    root.add_argument("--root", type=Path, default=Path("runs"), help="the runs folder")
+    runs_of = argparse.ArgumentParser(add_help=False, parents=[root])
+    runs_of.add_argument("world")
+    runs_of.add_argument("variant")
+    runs_of.add_argument("--include", action="append", default=[], metavar="NAME")
+
+    check = sub.add_parser("check", help="run the method check of a config")
+    check.add_argument("config", type=Path)
+    check.add_argument("--seed", type=int, default=0)
+    check.set_defaults(entry=_check)
+
+    train = sub.add_parser("train", parents=[root], help="train one seed of a config")
+    train.add_argument("config", type=Path)
+    train.add_argument("--seed", type=int, required=True)
+    train.add_argument("--check-seed", type=int, default=0)
+    train.set_defaults(entry=_train)
+
+    batch = sub.add_parser("batch", parents=[root], help="train several seeds of a config")
+    batch.add_argument("config", type=Path)
+    batch.add_argument("--seeds", type=parse_seeds, required=True, help="1-5 or 1,3,5")
+    batch.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
+    batch.add_argument("--check-seed", type=int, default=0)
+    batch.set_defaults(entry=_batch)
+
+    watch = sub.add_parser("watch", help="show a run's progress")
+    watch.add_argument("run_path", type=Path)
+    watch.set_defaults(entry=_watch)
+
+    chart = sub.add_parser("chart", parents=[runs_of], help="draw a world's learning curves")
+    chart.add_argument("--measure", default=None)
+    chart.set_defaults(entry=_chart)
+
+    evaluate = sub.add_parser("evaluate", parents=[runs_of], help="evaluate a world's runs")
+    evaluate.add_argument("--workers", type=int, default=None)
+    evaluate.set_defaults(entry=_evaluate)
+    return parser
+
+
+def _show(result: object) -> None:
+    for item in result if isinstance(result, list) else [result]:
+        print(item)
+
+
+def main(argv: Sequence[str] | None = None) -> int:
+    args = _parser().parse_args(argv)
+    try:
+        _show(args.entry(args))
+    except Exception as exc:  # every error is one line naming its cause (cli.main)
+        print(f"rllab {args.command}: {type(exc).__name__}: {exc}", file=sys.stderr)
+        return 1
+    return 0

+"""Types of the final evaluation: comparisons, verdicts and summaries."""
+
+from collections.abc import Mapping
+from dataclasses import dataclass
+from typing import Literal
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Comparison:
+    """One learned-versus-baseline test; `difference` > 0 means better in the team's direction."""
+
+    baseline: Literal["random", "rule"]
+    team: str
+    learned_mean: float
+    baseline_mean: float
+    difference: float
+    p_raw: float
+    p_holm: float
+    rejected: bool
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Verdict:
+    beats_baselines: bool
+    failing_runs: tuple[str, ...]
+    runs_needed: int
+
+
+@dataclass(frozen=True)
+class Summary:
+    """One world, variant and team's final evaluation (EVAL-06)."""
+
+    world: str
+    variant: str
+    team: str
+    runs: tuple[str, ...]
+    learned_mean: float
+    learned_range: tuple[float, float]
+    run_means: Mapping[str, float]
+    random_mean: float
+    rule_mean: float
+    comparisons: tuple[Comparison, ...]
+    verdict: Verdict
+    collapse: int | None

1	"""Shared by the contract tests, doubles and fakes: JSON samples, schemas and decoding.
2	
3	Folders are named `<component>.<Symbol>` and hold `schema.json` and `samples/*.json`; a
4	Protocol's folder also holds `double.py` (its replay double) and a boundary function's folder
5	`fake.py` (its fixture-backed fake). Nothing here is on `sys.path`: a test elsewhere imports this
6	file by path (`importlib.util.spec_from_file_location`, registering the module in `sys.modules`
7	before running it, as tests/contracts/conftest.py does), then asks it for what it needs:
8	
9	    kit.sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")   # a decoded WorldSpec
10	    kit.double("core.World", "snake_seed42")        # a fresh replay World
11	    kit.fake("train.run_train")                     # the fake, with the contract signature
12	
13	JSON encoding of the contract types: a dataclass is an object of its fields, a tuple or list an
14	array, a Mapping an object, an `Array` nested lists (floats decode as float32), a `Path` a string,
15	a `datetime` an ISO 8601 string and a `timedelta` its seconds.
16	"""
17	
18	from __future__ import annotations
19	
20	import dataclasses
21	import importlib
22	import importlib.util
23	import json
24	import sys
25	import types
26	import typing
27	from collections.abc import Mapping
28	from datetime import datetime, timedelta
29	from pathlib import Path
30	from typing import Any
31	
32	import numpy as np
33	
34	CONTRACTS = Path(__file__).parent
35	# A case-insensitive file system has one folder for `runlog.progress` and `runlog.Progress`, so
36	# the function's fake lives in its type's folder.
37	SHARED = {"runlog.progress": "runlog.Progress"}
38	
39	
40	def module(folder: str, name: str) -> types.ModuleType:
41	    """Import `tests/contracts/<folder>/<name>.py` by path, once."""
42	    key = f"contracts_{folder}_{name}".replace(".", "_")
43	    if key not in sys.modules:
44	        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(key, CONTRACTS / folder / f"{name}.py")
45	        assert spec is not None
46	        assert spec.loader is not None
47	        module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
48	        sys.modules[key] = module
49	        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
50	    return sys.modules[key]
51	
52	
53	def read(folder: str, sample: str) -> Any:
54	    """The JSON of `tests/contracts/<folder>/samples/<sample>.json`."""
55	    return json.loads((CONTRACTS / folder / "samples" / f"{sample}.json").read_text())
56	
57	
58	def sample(folder: str, name: str, tp: Any = None) -> Any:
59	    """A sample decoded as `tp`, by default the contract type the folder is named after."""
60	    return decode(tp if tp is not None else contract_type(folder), read(folder, name))
61	
62	
63	def contract_type(folder: str) -> Any:
64	    """`core.WorldSpec` -> `rllab.core.WorldSpec`."""
65	    component, symbol = folder.split(".")
66	    return getattr(importlib.import_module(f"rllab.{component}"), symbol)
67	
68	
69	def double(folder: str, name: str) -> Any:
70	    """A fresh replay double of the Protocol `folder`, serving the recording `name`."""
71	    return module(folder, "double").build(sys.modules[__name__], read(folder, name))
72	
73	
74	def fake(symbol: str) -> Any:
75	    """The fake of the boundary function `symbol`, with its contract signature."""
76	    return module(SHARED.get(symbol, symbol), "fake").build(sys.modules[__name__])
77	
78	
79	def faked() -> list[str]:
80	    """Every boundary function with a fake."""
81	    owner = {folder: symbol for symbol, folder in SHARED.items()}
82	    return sorted(owner.get(p.parent.name, p.parent.name) for p in CONTRACTS.glob("*/fake.py"))
83	
84	
85	def samples(folder: str) -> list[str]:
86	    return sorted(p.stem for p in (CONTRACTS / folder / "samples").glob("*.json"))
87	
88	
89	def schema(folder: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
90	    return json.loads((CONTRACTS / folder / "schema.json").read_text())
91	
92	
93	def folders() -> list[str]:
94	    return sorted(p.parent.name for p in CONTRACTS.glob("*/schema.json"))
95	
96	
97	def encode(value: Any) -> Any:
98	    """A contract value as JSON data, the inverse of `decode`."""
99	    if dataclasses.is_dataclass(value) and not isinstance(value, type):
100	        return {f.name: encode(getattr(value, f.name)) for f in dataclasses.fields(value)}
101	    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
102	        return {encode(k): encode(v) for k, v in value.items()}
103	    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple, np.ndarray)):
104	        return [encode(v) for v in value]
105	    if isinstance(value, np.generic):
106	        return value.item()
107	    return _encode_scalar(value)
108	
109	
110	def _encode_scalar(value: Any) -> Any:
111	    if isinstance(value, Path):
112	        return str(value)
113	    if isinstance(value, datetime):
114	        return value.isoformat()
115	    if isinstance(value, timedelta):
116	        return value.total_seconds()
117	    return value
118	
119	
120	# ------------------------------------------------------------------- JSON Schema (a subset)
121	_JSON_TYPES: dict[str, tuple[type, ...]] = {
122	    "object": (dict,),
123	    "array": (list,),
124	    "string": (str,),
125	    "integer": (int,),
126	    "number": (int, float),
127	    "boolean": (bool,),
128	    "null": (type(None),),
129	}
130	
131	
132	def errors(instance: Any, rules: Mapping[str, Any], where: str = "$") -> list[str]:
133	    """Every way `instance` breaks `rules`: type, enum, const, minimum, maximum, minItems,
134	    maxItems, items, properties, required, additionalProperties, anyOf, and `$ref` to another
135	    folder's schema by name (`"$ref": "core.TeamSpec"`, or one of its properties with
136	    `"$ref": "core.Policy#calls"`)."""
137	    if "$ref" in rules:
138	        folder, _, prop = rules["$ref"].partition("#")
139	        target = schema(folder)
140	        return errors(instance, target["properties"][prop] if prop else target, where)
141	    if "anyOf" in rules:
142	        if all(errors(instance, option, where) for option in rules["anyOf"]):
143	            return [f"{where}: matches no anyOf option"]
144	        return []
145	    if "type" in rules and not _type_ok(instance, rules["type"]):
146	        return [f"{where}: {instance!r} is not {rules['type']}"]
147	    found = _value_errors(instance, rules, where)
148	    if isinstance(instance, list):
149	        found += _array_errors(instance, rules, where)
150	    if isinstance(instance, dict):
151	        found += _object_errors(instance, rules, where)
152	    return found
153	
154	
155	def _type_ok(instance: Any, wanted: str | list[str]) -> bool:
156	    names = [wanted] if isinstance(wanted, str) else wanted
157	    if isinstance(instance, bool):
158	        return "boolean" in names
159	    return any(isinstance(instance, _JSON_TYPES[n]) for n in names)
160	
161	
162	def _value_errors(instance: Any, rules: Mapping[str, Any], where: str) -> list[str]:
163	    found = []
164	    if "enum" in rules and instance not in rules["enum"]:
165	        found.append(f"{where}: {instance!r} not in {rules['enum']}")
166	    if "const" in rules and instance != rules["const"]:
167	        found.append(f"{where}: {instance!r} != {rules['const']!r}")
168	    if "minimum" in rules and instance < rules["minimum"]:
169	        found.append(f"{where}: {instance!r} < {rules['minimum']}")
170	    if "maximum" in rules and instance > rules["maximum"]:
171	        found.append(f"{where}: {instance!r} > {rules['maximum']}")
172	    return found
173	
174	
175	def _array_errors(instance: list[Any], rules: Mapping[str, Any], where: str) -> list[str]:
176	    found = []
177	    if len(instance) < rules.get("minItems", 0):
178	        found.append(f"{where}: fewer than {rules['minItems']} items")
179	    if len(instance) > rules.get("maxItems", len(instance)):
180	        found.append(f"{where}: more than {rules['maxItems']} items")
181	    for i, item in enumerate(instance):
182	        found += errors(item, rules.get("items", {}), f"{where}[{i}]")
183	    return found
184	
185	
186	def _object_errors(instance: dict[str, Any], rules: Mapping[str, Any], where: str) -> list[str]:
187	    props = rules.get("properties", {})
188	    found = [f"{where}: missing {k!r}" for k in rules.get("required", []) if k not in instance]
189	    for key, value in instance.items():
190	        if key in props:
191	            found += errors(value, props[key], f"{where}.{key}")
192	        elif rules.get("additionalProperties", True) is False:
193	            found.append(f"{where}: unexpected {key!r}")
194	        elif isinstance(rules.get("additionalProperties"), dict):
195	            found += errors(value, rules["additionalProperties"], f"{where}.{key}")
196	    return found
197	
198	
199	# ------------------------------------------------------------------ decoding to contract types
200	def decode(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
201	    """`data` (parsed JSON) as an instance of the contract type `tp`; TypeError if it isn't one."""
202	    if isinstance(tp, typing.TypeAliasType):
203	        tp = tp.__value__
204	    if tp is Any or tp is object:
205	        return data
206	    decoder = next((d for test, d in _DECODERS if test(tp)), _decode_scalar)
207	    return decoder(tp, data)
208	
209	
210	def _decode_dataclass(tp: type, data: Any) -> Any:
211	    fields = {f.name: f for f in dataclasses.fields(tp)}
212	    required = {
213	        n
214	        for n, f in fields.items()
215	        if f.default is dataclasses.MISSING and f.default_factory is dataclasses.MISSING
216	    }
217	    if not isinstance(data, dict) or not required <= data.keys() <= fields.keys():
218	        raise TypeError(f"{tp.__name__} needs an object of {sorted(fields)}, got {data!r}")
219	    hints = typing.get_type_hints(tp)
220	    return tp(**{k: decode(hints[k], v) for k, v in data.items()})
221	
222	
223	def _decode_union(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
224	    options = typing.get_args(tp)
225	    if data is None and type(None) in options:
226	        return None
227	    problems = []
228	    for option in (o for o in options if o is not type(None)):
229	        try:
230	            return decode(option, data)
231	        except TypeError as exc:
232	            problems.append(str(exc))
233	    raise TypeError(f"{data!r} matches no option: {problems}")
234	
235	
236	def _decode_literal(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
237	    if data not in typing.get_args(tp):
238	        raise TypeError(f"{data!r} is not one of {typing.get_args(tp)}")
239	    return data
240	
241	
242	def _is_array(tp: Any) -> bool:
243	    """`np.ndarray`, `np.ndarray[...]`, or a subscripted alias of it such as `NDArray[...]`."""
244	    origin = typing.get_origin(tp)
245	    if isinstance(origin, typing.TypeAliasType):
246	        origin = typing.get_origin(origin.__value__)
247	    return tp is np.ndarray or origin is np.ndarray
248	
249	
250	def _decode_array(_tp: Any, data: Any) -> np.ndarray[Any, Any]:
251	    array = np.asarray(data)
252	    if array.dtype.kind == "f":
253	        return array.astype(np.float32)
254	    if array.dtype.kind not in "biu":
255	        raise TypeError(f"{data!r} is not a numeric array")
256	    return array
257	
258	
259	def _decode_generic(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
260	    origin, args = typing.get_origin(tp), typing.get_args(tp)
261	    is_mapping = isinstance(origin, type) and issubclass(origin, Mapping)
262	    if not isinstance(data, dict if is_mapping else list):
263	        raise TypeError(f"{tp!r} can't hold {data!r}")
264	    if is_mapping:
265	        return {decode(args[0], k): decode(args[1], v) for k, v in data.items()}
266	    if origin is list or (len(args) == 2 and args[1] is Ellipsis):
267	        items = [decode(args[0], d) for d in data]
268	        return items if origin is list else tuple(items)
269	    if len(args) != len(data):
270	        raise TypeError(f"{data!r} does not have {len(args)} items")
271	    return tuple(decode(a, d) for a, d in zip(args, data, strict=True))
272	
273	
274	def _decode_scalar(tp: Any, data: Any) -> Any:
275	    if tp is Path:
276	        return Path(_checked(str, data))
277	    if tp is datetime:
278	        return datetime.fromisoformat(_checked(str, data))
279	    if tp is timedelta:
280	        return timedelta(seconds=_checked(float, data))
281	    if tp is float:
282	        return float(_checked(float, data))
283	    if tp in {int, str, bool}:
284	        return _checked(tp, data)
285	    raise TypeError(f"no decoder for {tp!r}")
286	
287	
288	def _checked(tp: type, data: Any) -> Any:
289	    numeric = tp is float and isinstance(data, int)
290	    if (isinstance(data, bool) and tp is not bool) or not (isinstance(data, tp) or numeric):
291	        raise TypeError(f"{data!r} is not {tp.__name__}")
292	    return data
293	
294	
295	_DECODERS: list[tuple[typing.Callable[[Any], bool], typing.Callable[[Any, Any], Any]]] = [
296	    (lambda tp: isinstance(tp, type) and dataclasses.is_dataclass(tp), _decode_dataclass),
297	    (lambda tp: typing.get_origin(tp) in {types.UnionType, typing.Union}, _decode_union),
298	    (lambda tp: typing.get_origin(tp) is typing.Literal, _decode_literal),
299	    (_is_array, _decode_array),
300	    (lambda tp: typing.get_origin(tp) is not None, _decode_generic),
301	]

90	def bare(fn: Callable[..., Any]) -> str:
91	    """The signature without annotations: names, kinds and defaults."""
92	    sig = inspect.signature(fn)
93	    params = [p.replace(annotation=inspect.Parameter.empty) for p in sig.parameters.values()]
94	    return str(sig.replace(parameters=params, return_annotation=inspect.Signature.empty))
95	
96	
97	@pytest.mark.parametrize(("component", "names"), EXPORTS.items())
98	def test_component_exports_its_contract(component: str, names: set[str]) -> None:
99	    module = importlib.import_module(f"rllab.{component}")
100	    assert set(module.__all__) == names
101	    assert all(hasattr(module, name) for name in names)
102	
103	
104	@pytest.mark.parametrize(("module", "name", "signature"), SIGNATURES)
105	def test_step_has_its_contract_signature(module: str, name: str, signature: str) -> None:
106	    assert bare(getattr(importlib.import_module(f"rllab.{module}"), name)) == signature
107	
108	
109	def test_every_boundary_function_has_a_fake(kit: Any) -> None:
110	    assert kit.faked() == FAKES
111	
112	
113	@pytest.mark.parametrize("symbol", FAKES)
114	def test_fake_has_the_signature_of_what_it_fakes(kit: Any, symbol: str) -> None:
115	    assert bare(kit.fake(symbol)) == bare(kit.contract_type(symbol))

{
  "file_path": "/Users/connorott/plugins/dev-team-workspace/iteration-b1/rl9-all-at-once-A3-r2/with_skill/project/.claude/worktrees/agent-ab701912abb3c8706/tests/contracts/core.World/double.py"

1	"""Replay double of `core.World`: serves one recorded episode.
2	
3	`reset(seed)` returns the recorded start for any seed (and `result()` reports that seed), so a
4	training loop can reset it as often as it likes. `step` refuses an action outside `spec.action`
5	with `ActionError` and a step after `done` with `RuntimeError`, as every World must; any action
6	inside the action space advances the recording by one step.
7	"""
8	
9	from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
10	from typing import Any
11	
12	import numpy as np
13	
14	from rllab.core import ActionError, Array, Box, DrawState, EpisodeResult, Step, WorldSpec
15	
16	
17	def check_actions(spec: WorldSpec, actions: Array, active: Array) -> None:
18	    """ActionError for the first active agent whose action is outside `spec.action`."""
19	    for i, row in enumerate(np.asarray(actions)):
20	        if active[i] and not inside(spec, row):
21	            raise ActionError(spec.agents[i], row.tolist())
22	
23	
24	def inside(spec: WorldSpec, action: Array) -> bool:
25	    if isinstance(spec.action, Box):
26	        low, high = spec.action.low, spec.action.high
27	        return action.shape == (spec.action.dims,) and bool(
28	            np.all((action >= low) & (action <= high))
29	        )
30	    return action.shape == () and float(action).is_integer() and 0 <= action < spec.action.n
31	
32	
33	@dataclass
34	class ReplayWorld:
35	    spec: WorldSpec
36	    steps: list[Step]
37	    draws: list[DrawState]
38	    recorded: EpisodeResult
39	    at: int | None = field(default=None, init=False)
40	    seed: int = field(default=0, init=False)
41	
42	    def reset(self, seed: int) -> Step:
43	        self.at, self.seed = 0, seed
44	        return self.steps[0]
45	
46	    def step(self, actions: Array) -> Step:
47	        if self.at is None or self.steps[self.at].done:
48	            raise RuntimeError("step before reset or after done")
49	        check_actions(self.spec, actions, self.steps[self.at].active)
50	        self.at += 1
51	        return self.steps[self.at]
52	
53	    def result(self) -> EpisodeResult:
54	        return replace(self.recorded, seed=self.seed)
55	
56	    def draw(self) -> DrawState:
57	        return self.draws[self.at or 0]
58	
59	
60	def build(kit: Any, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> ReplayWorld:
61	    return ReplayWorld(
62	        kit.decode(WorldSpec, recording["spec"]),
63	        kit.decode(list[Step], recording["steps"]),
64	        kit.decode(list[DrawState], recording["draws"]),
65	        kit.decode(EpisodeResult, recording["result"]),
66	    )
67

2	episode. Each test takes its provider as a fixture, so a real World, Policy or play_episode can
3	run the same checks."""

5	from typing import Any

8	import pytest

16	    return replay

26	    return kit.fake("core.play_episode")

28	
29	def zero_actions(spec: WorldSpec) -> np.ndarray[Any, Any]:
30	    n = len(spec.agents)
31	    return (
32	        np.zeros(n, int) if isinstance(spec.action, Discrete) else np.zeros((n, spec.action.dims))
33	    )
34	
35	
36	def outside_actions(spec: WorldSpec) -> np.ndarray[Any, Any]:
37	    if isinstance(spec.action, Discrete):
38	        return np.full(len(spec.agents), spec.action.n)
39	    return np.full((len(spec.agents), spec.action.dims), spec.action.high + 0.5)
40

42	def test_world_double_replays_its_recording(kit: Any, world: World) -> None:

44	    steps = kit.decode(list[Step], recording["steps"])
45	    assert np.array_equal(world.reset(recording["seed"]).obs, steps[0].obs)
46	    for action, recorded in zip(recording["actions"], steps[1:], strict=True):
47	        step = world.step(np.asarray(action))
48	        assert np.array_equal(step.obs, recorded.obs)
49	        assert np.array_equal(step.rewards, recorded.rewards)
50	    assert step.done

58	    assert step.obs.dtype == np.float32

60	    assert step.active.dtype == np.bool_

72	        world.step(outside_actions(world.spec))
73	    assert isinstance(raised.value, ValueError)

77	def test_step_after_done_raises_runtime_error(world: World) -> None:
78	    step = world.reset(42)
79	    for _ in range(world.spec.max_steps):
80	        if step.done:

82	        step = world.step(zero_actions(world.spec))
83	    assert step.done
84	    with pytest.raises(RuntimeError, match="done"):
85	        world.step(zero_actions(world.spec))

14	def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[..., Episode]:
15	    def play_episode(
16	        world: World, policies: Mapping[str, Policy], seed: int, *, trace: bool = False
17	    ) -> Episode:
18	        for team in world.spec.teams:
19	            if team.name not in policies:

22	            raise LookupError(f"the fake recorded seed 7, not {seed}")
23	        episode: Episode = kit.sample("core.play_episode", "snake_rule_seed7", Episode)

1	"""Replay double of `core.Policy`: acts as recorded, one observation row at a time.
2	
3	An observation it never saw raises `KeyError`: the double knows only its recording.
4	"""
5	
6	from typing import Any
7	
8	import numpy as np
9	
10	from rllab.core import Array
11	
12	
13	def row_key(row: Array) -> tuple[float, ...]:
14	    return tuple(np.round(np.asarray(row, np.float64), 5).tolist())
15	
16	
17	class ReplayPolicy:
18	    def __init__(self, calls: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> None:
19	        self.table: dict[tuple[float, ...], Array] = {}
20	        for call in calls:
21	            for row, action in zip(call["obs"], call["actions"], strict=True):
22	                self.table[row_key(np.asarray(row))] = np.asarray(action)
23	
24	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array:
25	        return np.stack([self.table[row_key(row)] for row in obs])
26	
27	
28	def build(kit: Any, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> ReplayPolicy:
29	    return ReplayPolicy(recording["calls"])

106	class Board:
107	    """A 10 x 10 snake that never dies, for consistent recordings."""
108	
109	    def __init__(self) -> None:
110	        self.body = [(5, 5), (5, 6), (5, 7)]
111	        self.heading = (0, -1)
112	        self.foods = [(7, 3), (2, 2), (8, 8)]
113	        self.eaten = 0
114	
115	    def turned(self, action: int) -> tuple[int, int]:
116	        dx, dy = self.heading
117	        return [(dx, dy), (dy, -dx), (-dy, dx)][action]
118	
119	    def fatal(self, cell: tuple[int, int]) -> bool:
120	        x, y = cell
121	        return not (0 <= x < 10 and 0 <= y < 10) or cell in self.body[:-1]
122	
123	    def obs(self) -> list[list[float]]:
124	        hx, hy = self.body[0]
125	        turns = [self.turned(a) for a in range(3)]
126	        danger = [float(self.fatal((hx + dx, hy + dy))) for dx, dy in turns]
127	        dirs = [float(self.heading == d) for d in [(0, -1), (1, 0), (0, 1), (-1, 0)]]
128	        fx, fy = self.foods[0]
129	        food = [float(fy < hy), float(fx > hx), float(fy > hy), float(fx < hx)]
130	        return [danger + dirs + food]
131	
132	    def step(self, action: int) -> float:
133	        self.heading = self.turned(action)
134	        head = (self.body[0][0] + self.heading[0], self.body[0][1] + self.heading[1])
135	        assert not self.fatal(head), "the recording never dies"
136	        self.body.insert(0, head)
137	        if head == self.foods[0]:
138	            self.foods.pop(0)
139	            self.eaten += 1
140	            return 1.0
141	        self.body.pop()
142	        return 0.0
143	
144	    def draw(self) -> DrawState:
145	        cells = [
146	            Drawable("snake-head" if i == 0 else "snake-body", float(x), float(y), 1.0, "snake", 0)
147	            for i, (x, y) in enumerate(self.body)
148	        ]
149	        fx, fy = self.foods[0]
150	        food = Drawable("food", float(fx), float(fy), 1.0, None, None)
151	        return DrawState(10.0, 10.0, (*cells, food))
152	
153	
154	def consistent(calls: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
155	    """A policy recording never answers one observation two ways (core.Policy invariant)."""
156	    seen: dict[bytes, list[int]] = {}
157	    for call in calls:
158	        key = np.asarray(call["obs"], np.float32).tobytes()
159	        assert seen.setdefault(key, list(call["actions"])) == list(call["actions"]), call
160	    return calls
161	
162	
163	def one_step(obs: list[list[float]], reward: float, last: bool = False) -> Step:
164	    return Step(
165	        np.array(obs, np.float32), np.array([reward], np.float32), np.array([True]), last, last
166	    )
167	
168	
169	def snake_seed42() -> tuple[list[Step], list[DrawState], list[float], EpisodeResult]:
170	    """`make("snake", max_steps=5)` reset from seed 42, played by the rule."""
171	    board = Board()
172	    steps, draws, rewards = [one_step(board.obs(), 0.0)], [board.draw()], []
173	    for i, action in enumerate(ACTIONS_42):
174	        rewards.append(board.step(action))
175	        steps.append(one_step(board.obs(), rewards[-1], last=i == len(ACTIONS_42) - 1))
176	        draws.append(board.draw())
177	    result = EpisodeResult(42, 5, {"snake": float(board.eaten)}, {"snake": sum(rewards)}, {})
178	    return steps, draws, rewards, result

181	def write_random_policy() -> None:
182	    """Five `act` calls of one agent each, from `np.random.default_rng(seed)`."""
183	    snake = np.random.default_rng(5).integers(0, 3, (5, 1))
184	    write("baselines.RandomPolicy", "snake_5", {"action": Discrete(3), "seed": 5, "actions": snake})
185	    pushes = np.round(np.random.default_rng(6).uniform(-1, 1, (5, 1, 2)), 4)
186	    fish = {"action": Box(2, -1.0, 1.0), "seed": 6, "actions": pushes}
187	    write("baselines.RandomPolicy", "fish_5", fish)

190	def write_fish_rule(rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:
191	    fish_obs = np.round(rng.uniform(-1, 1, (1, 20)), 3)
192	    fish_obs[0, 4:7] = 0.0  # the ball-seen flag and the ball's offset: not seen
193	    fish_rule = (
194	        "Pushes at full strength straight away from the ball when it sees it, and otherwise "
195	        "pushes in a random direction."
196	    )
197	    fish_call = {"obs": fish_obs, "actions": [[0.31, -0.74]]}
198	    write("baselines.RulePolicy", "fish_no_ball", {"description": fish_rule, "calls": [fish_call]})

201	def write_snake_recordings() -> None:
202	    steps, draws, rewards, result = snake_seed42()
203	    world = {"spec": replace(SNAKE, max_steps=5), "seed": 42, "actions": [[a] for a in ACTIONS_42]}
204	    write("core.World", "snake_seed42", {**world, "steps": steps, "draws": draws, "result": result})
205	    calls = consistent(
206	        [{"obs": s.obs, "actions": [a]} for s, a in zip(steps, ACTIONS_42, strict=False)]
207	    )
208	    write("core.Policy", "snake_rule", {"calls": calls})
209	    snake_rule = (
210	        "Turns toward the food along the shorter axis, unless that move would end the episode "
211	        "on the next step."
212	    )
213	    write("baselines.RulePolicy", "snake_rule", {"description": snake_rule, "calls": calls})
214	    scores = np.concatenate([[0.0], np.cumsum(rewards)])
215	    latest = [0.0, *rewards]
216	    frames = tuple(
217	        Frame(i, d, {"snake": float(scores[i])}, np.array([latest[i]], np.float32))
218	        for i, d in enumerate(draws)
219	    )
220	    write("core.play_episode", "snake_rule_seed7", Episode(replace(result, seed=7), frames, True))
221	    write("core.Frame", "snake_seed7_frame0", frames[0])
222	    write("core.Frame", "snake_seed7_frame1", frames[1])
223	    write("core.Step", "snake_mid", steps[2])
224	    write("core.DrawState", "snake_board", draws[3])

227	def onehot(i: int | None) -> list[float]:
228	    return [float(i == j) for j in range(6)]
229

231	def corridor_draw(cell: int | None) -> DrawState:
232	    exits = (
233	        Drawable("exit", -1.0, 0.0, 1.0, None, None),
234	        Drawable("exit", 6.0, 0.0, 1.0, None, None),
235	    )
236	    agent = () if cell is None else (Drawable("agent", float(cell), 0.0, 1.0, "agent", 0),)
237	    return DrawState(8.0, 1.0, (*exits, *agent))
