357	def snake_transitions() -> list[Transition]:
358	    """Ten exploring DQN steps from the seed-42 start."""
359	    board, transitions = Board(), []
360	    for a in EXPLORE:
361	        obs = np.array(board.obs(), np.float32)
362	        reward = np.array([board.step(a)], np.float32)
363	        next_obs = np.array(board.obs(), np.float32)
364	        t = Transition(obs, np.array([a]), reward, next_obs, False, False, np.array([True]))
365	        transitions.append(t)
366	    return transitions
367	
368	
369	def write_methods(rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:
370	    transitions = snake_transitions()
371	    acts = consistent([{"obs": t.obs, "actions": t.actions} for t in transitions])
372	    policy = consistent(
373	        [{"obs": t.obs, "actions": [g]} for t, g in zip(transitions, GREEDY, strict=True)]
374	    )
375	    state = {
376	        "net.0.weight": np.round(rng.normal(0, 0.3, (4, 11)), 4),
377	        "net.0.bias": np.zeros(4),
378	        "net.2.weight": np.round(rng.normal(0, 0.5, (3, 4)), 4),
379	        "net.2.bias": np.zeros(3),
380	        "updates": 10,
381	    }
382	    learner = {"acts": acts, "transitions": transitions, "policy": policy, "state_dict": state}
383	    write("methods.Learner", "dqn_snake_10", learner)
384	    write("methods.Transition", "snake", transitions[3])
385	
386	
387	def write_fish_transition(rng: np.random.Generator) -> None:
388	    fish = np.round(rng.uniform(-1, 1, (8, 20)), 3).astype(np.float32)
389	    moved = np.round(fish + rng.normal(0, 0.02, (8, 20)), 3).astype(np.float32)
390	    pushes = np.round(rng.uniform(-1, 1, (8, 2)), 3).astype(np.float32)
391	    hits = np.array([0, 0, -1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0], np.float32)
392	    write(
393	        "methods.Transition",
394	        "fish_many_8",
395	        Transition(fish, pushes, hits, moved, False, False, np.ones(8, bool)),
396	    )
397	
398	
399	def snake_config(seed: int, budget: int = 2000, name: str | None = None) -> RunConfig:
400	    name = name or f"snake-s{seed}"
401	    return RunConfig(
402	        name, "snake", "default", {"snake": DQN}, seed, budget, {"dqn": {"hidden": 32}}
403	    )
404	
405	
406	def record(seed: int, status: str = "finished", budget: int = 2000) -> RunRecord:
407	    config = snake_config(seed, budget)
408	    ended = None if status == "incomplete" else T0 + timedelta(minutes=seed, seconds=90)
409	    path = Path("runs/snake/default") / config.name
410	    started = T0 + timedelta(minutes=seed)
411	    return RunRecord(config, SNAKE, started, ended, status, None, path)
412	
413	
414	def run(seed: int, status: str = "finished") -> dict[str, object]:
415	    rec = record(seed, status)
416	    gen = np.random.default_rng(seed)
417	    episodes, step = [], 0
418	    while step < 1800:
419	        length = int(gen.integers(120, 300))
420	        food = float(min(step // 400 + int(gen.integers(0, 2)), 6))
421	        episodes.append(
422	            EpisodeResult(
423	                int(gen.integers(0, 1_000_000)), length, {"snake": food}, {"snake": food}, {}
424	            )
425	        )
426	        step += length
427	    last = 2000 if status == "finished" else 1200
428	    evals = [
429	        EvalPoint(s, {"snake": round(s / 400 + 0.1 * seed, 3)}, {}) for s in range(0, last + 1, 100)
430	    ]
431	    ckpts = [
432	        Checkpoint(
433	            rec.config.name,
434	            pct,
435	            pct * 20,
436	            SNAKE,
437	            {"snake": DQN},
438	            {"snake": {"net.0.bias": [round(0.01 * pct, 3)] * 4, "updates": pct * 20}},
439	            rec.path / "checkpoints" / f"ckpt_{pct}.pt",
440	        )
441	        for pct in (0, 50, 100)
442	        if pct * 20 <= last
443	    ]
444	    return {"record": rec, "episodes": episodes, "evals": evals, "checkpoints": ckpts}
445	
446	
447	def write_train_and_runlog() -> None:
448	    write("train.CheckResult", "dqn_pass", CheckResult("dqn", "check-discrete", 1.0, True))
449	    write("train.CheckResult", "ppo_fail", CheckResult("ppo", "check-continuous", 0.8, False))
450	    write("train.load_run_configs", "snake_s1_s2", [snake_config(1), snake_config(2)])
451	    write("train.run_batch", "two_finished", [record(1), record(2)])
452	    finished = run(1)
453	    write("runlog.Run", "snake_finished", finished)
454	    runs = [run(s) for s in range(1, 6)] + [run(6, "incomplete")]
455	    write("runlog.find_runs", "snake_5_plus_incomplete", runs)
456	    writes = {"root": "runs", "config": snake_config(1), "spec": SNAKE}
457	    write(
458	        "runlog.RunWriter",
459	        "snake_3_checkpoints",
460	        {
461	            **writes,
462	            **{k: v for k, v in finished.items() if k != "record"},
463	            "record": finished["record"],
464	        },
465	    )
466	    write("runlog.RunRecord", "snake_finished", finished["record"])
467	    failed = record(4, "failed", 200_000)
468	    nan = Failure(41_200, math.nan, failed.path / "checkpoints" / "ckpt_20.pt")
469	    write("runlog.RunRecord", "snake_failed_nan", replace(failed, failure=nan))
470	    write("runlog.RunRecord", "snake_incomplete", record(3, "incomplete"))
471	    write("runlog.EvalPoint", "snake_50pct", EvalPoint(100_000, {"snake": 4.2}, {}))
472	    both = EvalPoint(500_000, {"prey": 1.3, "predator": 2.0}, {"prey survivors": 3.5})
473	    write("runlog.EvalPoint", "prey_both_teams", both)
474	    checkpoints = finished["checkpoints"]
475	    assert isinstance(checkpoints, list)
476	    write("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_0pct", checkpoints[0])
477	    write("runlog.Checkpoint", "snake_100pct", checkpoints[-1])
478	    latest = EvalPoint(70_000, {"snake": 3.1}, {})
479	    write(
480	        "runlog.Progress",
481	        "snake_35pct",
482	        Progress(70_000, 200_000, latest, timedelta(seconds=1260), "running"),
483	    )
484	
485	
486	def write_evaluate() -> None:
487	    random_rejected = Comparison("random", "snake", 12.42, 0.2, 12.22, 0.0001, 0.0002, True)
488	    rule_rejected = Comparison("rule", "snake", 12.42, 9.5, 2.92, 0.004, 0.004, True)
489	    write("evaluate.Comparison", "snake_random_rejected", random_rejected)
490	    write(
491	        "evaluate.Comparison",
492	        "snake_rule_kept",
493	        Comparison("rule", "snake", 9.8, 9.5, 0.3, 0.21, 0.21, False),
494	    )
495	    names = tuple(f"snake-s{i}" for i in range(1, 6))
496	    means = dict(zip(names, [11.9, 12.4, 13.1, 12.0, 12.7], strict=True))
497	    passing = Summary(
498	        "snake",
499	        "default",
500	        "snake",
501	        names,
502	        12.42,
503	        (11.9, 13.1),
504	        means,
505	        0.2,
506	        9.5,
507	        (random_rejected, rule_rejected),
508	        Verdict(True, (), 0),
509	        None,
510	    )
511	    write("evaluate.Summary", "snake_pass", passing)
512	    tests = (
513	        replace(random_rejected, learned_mean=11.7, difference=11.5),
514	        replace(rule_rejected, learned_mean=11.7, difference=2.2, p_raw=0.011, p_holm=0.011),
515	    )
516	    failing = replace(
517	        passing,
518	        learned_mean=11.7,
519	        learned_range=(9.1, 13.1),
520	        run_means={**means, "snake-s5": 9.1},
521	        comparisons=tests,
522	        verdict=Verdict(False, ("snake-s5",), 0),
523	    )
524	    write("evaluate.Summary", "snake_failing_run", failing)
525	    three = {n: means[n] for n in names[:3]}
526	    few = replace(
527	        passing,
528	        runs=names[:3],
529	        learned_mean=12.47,
530	        run_means=three,
531	        comparisons=(),
532	        verdict=Verdict(False, (), 2),
533	    )
534	    write("evaluate.Summary", "snake_three_runs", few)
535	
536	
537	def main() -> None:
538	    rng = np.random.default_rng(3)
539	    write_snake_recordings()
540	    write_fish_rule(rng)
541	    write_random_policy()
542	    write_check_worlds()
543	    write_core_types(rng)
544	    write_core_configs()
545	    write_methods(rng)
546	    write_fish_transition(rng)
547	    write_train_and_runlog()
548	    write_evaluate()
549	
550	
551	if __name__ == "__main__":
552	    main()

1	"""Replay double of `methods.Learner`: a DQN learner's recorded acts, policy and state.
2	
3	`act` answers each observation it recorded with its exploring action; `observe` keeps every
4	transition it is given (in `observed`); `policy()` is a frozen copy acting greedily as recorded.
5	"""
6	
7	from typing import Any
8	
9	import numpy as np
10	
11	from rllab.core import Array, Policy
12	from rllab.methods import Transition
13	
14	
15	class ReplayLearner:
16	    def __init__(self, kit: Any, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
17	        replay_policy = kit.module("core.Policy", "double").ReplayPolicy
18	        self._explore = replay_policy(recording["acts"])
19	        self._greedy = recording["policy"]
20	        self._replay_policy = replay_policy
21	        self._state = dict(recording["state_dict"])
22	        self.observed: list[Transition] = []
23	
24	    def act(self, obs: Array, rng: np.random.Generator) -> Array:
25	        return self._explore.act(obs, rng)
26	
27	    def observe(self, t: Transition) -> None:
28	        self.observed.append(t)
29	
30	    def policy(self) -> Policy:
31	        frozen: Policy = self._replay_policy(self._greedy)
32	        return frozen
33	
34	    def state_dict(self) -> dict[str, object]:
35	        return dict(self._state)
36	
37	
38	def build(kit: Any, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> ReplayLearner:
39	    return ReplayLearner(kit, recording)
40

1	"""Replay double of `runlog.RunWriter`: keeps what it is given, returns what the run recorded.
2	
3	`checkpoint(pct, ...)` returns the recorded checkpoint at that percentage (`KeyError` for one
4	the run never wrote); `close(status, failure)` returns the recorded record with that status and
5	failure. Nothing touches the disk.
6	"""
7	
8	from collections.abc import Mapping
9	from dataclasses import dataclass, field, replace
10	from typing import Any
11	
12	from rllab.core import EpisodeResult
13	from rllab.runlog import Checkpoint, EvalPoint, Failure, RunRecord
14	
15	
16	@dataclass
17	class ReplayRunWriter:
18	    recorded_checkpoints: dict[int, Checkpoint]
19	    recorded_record: RunRecord
20	    episodes: list[EpisodeResult] = field(default_factory=list)
21	    evals: list[EvalPoint] = field(default_factory=list)
22	    states: dict[int, Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
23	
24	    def episode(self, result: EpisodeResult) -> None:
25	        self.episodes.append(result)
26	
27	    def eval(self, point: EvalPoint) -> None:
28	        self.evals.append(point)
29	
30	    def checkpoint(self, pct: int, step: int, state: Mapping[str, dict[str, object]]) -> Checkpoint:
31	        self.states[pct] = state
32	        return self.recorded_checkpoints[pct]
33	
34	    def close(self, status: str, failure: Failure | None = None) -> RunRecord:
35	        return replace(self.recorded_record, status=status, failure=failure)
36	
37	
38	def build(kit: Any, recording: dict[str, Any]) -> ReplayRunWriter:
39	    checkpoints = kit.decode(list[Checkpoint], recording["checkpoints"])
40	    record = kit.decode(RunRecord, recording["record"])
41	    return ReplayRunWriter({c.pct: c for c in checkpoints}, record)

"""Fake of `train.run_train`: writes the finished 3-checkpoint snake run (runlog.Run sample).

The folder is `<root>/<world>/<variant>/<name>` with `run.json` (the record), `episodes.jsonl`,
`evals.jsonl` (one JSON object per line) and `checkpoints/ckpt_<pct>.pt` (`torch.save` of the
checkpoint's JSON form), as AD-06 lays it out. An existing folder raises `RunExistsError`
before anything is written, as `run_train` must.
"""

import json
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import torch

from rllab.core import RunConfig
from rllab.runlog import RunExistsError, RunRecord


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[[RunConfig, Path], RunRecord]:
    def run_train(config: RunConfig, root: Path) -> RunRecord:
        folder = root / config.world / config.variant / config.name
        if folder.exists():
            raise RunExistsError(folder)
        recording = kit.read("runlog.Run", "snake_finished")
        record = replace(kit.decode(RunRecord, recording["record"]), config=config, path=folder)
        (folder / "checkpoints").mkdir(parents=True)
        (folder / "run.json").write_text(json.dumps(kit.encode(record), indent=1))
        for log in ("episodes", "evals"):
            lines = [json.dumps(item) + "\n" for item in recording[log]]
            (folder / f"{log}.jsonl").write_text("".join(lines))
        for ckpt in recording["checkpoints"]:
            saved = {**ckpt, "run": config.name}
            torch.save(saved, folder / "checkpoints" / f"ckpt_{ckpt['pct']}.pt")
        return record

    return run_train
"""Fake of `runlog.find_runs`: 5 finished snake runs and one incomplete one (runlog.Run doubles).

As `find_runs` must, it returns the finished runs of the world and variant, plus the
incomplete or failed runs named in `include`.
"""

from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from rllab.runlog import Run


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[..., list[Run]]:
    def find_runs(
        root: Path, world: str, variant: str, *, include: Sequence[str] = ()
    ) -> list[Run]:
        replay = kit.module("runlog.Run", "double")
        runs: list[Run] = [
            replay.build(kit, r) for r in kit.read("runlog.find_runs", "snake_5_plus_incomplete")
        ]
        return [
            run
            for run in runs
            if (run.record.config.world, run.record.config.variant) == (world, variant)
            and (run.record.status == "finished" or run.record.config.name in include)
        ]

    return find_runs
"""Fake of `worlds.make`: `make("snake")` is the recorded seed-42 snake (core.World double).

The recording was made with the `max_steps=5` override, the one override the fake accepts.
Any other world, variant or override raises `ValueError` naming it, as `make` must.
"""

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

from rllab.core import World


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[..., World]:
    def make(world: str, variant: str = "default", **overrides: int | float) -> World:
        if world != "snake":
            raise ValueError(f"unknown world {world!r}")
        if variant != "default":
            raise ValueError(f"unknown variant {variant!r} of {world}")
        unknown = sorted(set(overrides) - {"max_steps"})
        if unknown:
            raise ValueError(f"unknown override {unknown[0]!r} of {world}")
        replay: World = kit.double("core.World", "snake_seed42")
        return replay

    return make
"""Fake of `methods.make_learner`: the recorded DQN learner (methods.Learner double).

`dqn` with a `Box` action space, or `ppo` with a `Discrete` one, raises `ValueError` naming the
method and the action format, as `make_learner` must. The fake records no PPO learner.
"""

from collections.abc import Callable
from typing import Any

import numpy as np

from rllab.core import MethodConfig, WorldSpec
from rllab.methods import Learner

NEEDS = {"dqn": "Discrete", "ppo": "Box"}


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[..., Learner]:
    def make_learner(
        config: MethodConfig, spec: WorldSpec, team: str, rng: np.random.Generator
    ) -> Learner:
        needed, found = NEEDS[config.method], type(spec.action).__name__
        if found != needed:
            raise ValueError(f"{config.method} needs a {needed} action space, not {found}")
        if config.method != "dqn":
            raise LookupError("the fake records only a DQN learner")
        learner: Learner = kit.double("methods.Learner", "dqn_snake_10")
        return learner

    return make_learner
"""Fake of `train.load_run_configs`: the recorded snake configs for seeds 1 and 2.

Each is named `<file stem>-s<seed>`; a missing file raises `FileNotFoundError`, as
`load_run_configs` must. A seed with no recorded config raises `LookupError`.
"""

from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import replace
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from rllab.core import RunConfig


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[[Path, Sequence[int]], list[RunConfig]]:
    def load_run_configs(path: Path, seeds: Sequence[int]) -> list[RunConfig]:
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"no config file at {path}")
        recorded = {
            c.seed: c for c in kit.sample("train.load_run_configs", "snake_s1_s2", list[RunConfig])
        }
        missing = [s for s in seeds if s not in recorded]
        if missing:
            raise LookupError(f"the fake recorded seeds 1 and 2, not {missing}")
        return [replace(recorded[s], name=f"{path.stem}-s{s}") for s in seeds]

    return load_run_configs
"""Fake of `runlog.progress`: a snake run at 35% with its 35% point written.

A path with no run raises `FileNotFoundError`, as `progress` must.
"""

from collections.abc import Callable
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from rllab.runlog import Progress


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[[Path], Progress]:
    def progress(path: Path) -> Progress:
        if not (path / "run.json").is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"no run at {path}")
        snapshot: Progress = kit.sample("runlog.Progress", "snake_35pct")
        return snapshot

    return progress

"""evaluate: Comparison and Summary samples keep their invariants; the final_evaluation fake."""

from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest

from rllab.evaluate import Comparison, Summary

SUMMARIES = ["snake_pass", "snake_failing_run", "snake_three_runs"]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["snake_random_rejected", "snake_rule_kept"])
def test_comparison_holm_never_lowers_p(kit: Any, name: str) -> None:
    comparison: Comparison = kit.sample("evaluate.Comparison", name)
    assert comparison.p_holm >= comparison.p_raw
    assert comparison.rejected == (comparison.p_holm < 0.05)
    assert comparison.difference == pytest.approx(
        comparison.learned_mean - comparison.baseline_mean
    )


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", SUMMARIES)
def test_summary_range_and_runs_match_its_run_means(kit: Any, name: str) -> None:
    summary: Summary = kit.sample("evaluate.Summary", name)
    means = summary.run_means.values()
    assert summary.learned_range == (min(means), max(means))
    assert set(summary.runs) == set(summary.run_means)
    assert summary.learned_mean == pytest.approx(sum(means) / len(means), abs=0.01)
    assert all(c.team == summary.team for c in summary.comparisons)


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", SUMMARIES)
def test_summary_tests_only_with_5_runs(kit: Any, name: str) -> None:
    summary: Summary = kit.sample("evaluate.Summary", name)
    few = len(summary.runs) < 5
    assert (summary.comparisons == ()) == few
    assert (summary.verdict.runs_needed > 0) == few
    assert not (few and summary.verdict.beats_baselines)


def test_failing_run_is_named_in_the_verdict(kit: Any) -> None:
    summary: Summary = kit.sample("evaluate.Summary", "snake_failing_run")
    below = [r for r, m in summary.run_means.items() if m <= summary.rule_mean]
    assert list(summary.verdict.failing_runs) == below
    assert not summary.verdict.beats_baselines


def test_final_evaluation_gives_one_summary_per_team(kit: Any, tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    runs = kit.fake("runlog.find_runs")(tmp_path, "snake", "default")
    summaries: list[Summary] = kit.fake("evaluate.final_evaluation")(runs, tmp_path)
    teams = [t.name for t in runs[0].record.spec.teams]
    assert [s.team for s in summaries] == teams
    assert all(len(s.comparisons) == 2 for s in summaries)

"""Fake of `charts.learning_curve`: writes a placeholder PNG at `out` and names its runs."""

from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from matplotlib import image

from rllab.core import BaselineMeans, Output
from rllab.runlog import Run


def build(kit: Any) -> Callable[..., Output]:
    def learning_curve(
        runs: Sequence[Run], baselines: BaselineMeans, out: Path, *, measure: str | None = None
    ) -> Output:
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        image.imsave(out, np.ones((4, 4, 3)))
        return Output(out, "chart", tuple(run.record.config.name for run in runs))

    return learning_curve

"""The contract tests' shared kit (tests/contracts/kit.py), loaded by path."""

import importlib.util
import sys
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest


def _load_kit() -> Any:
    if "contracts_kit" not in sys.modules:
        spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location(
            "contracts_kit", Path(__file__).parent / "kit.py"
        )
        assert spec is not None
        assert spec.loader is not None
        module = importlib.util.module_from_spec(spec)
        sys.modules["contracts_kit"] = module
        spec.loader.exec_module(module)
    return sys.modules["contracts_kit"]


@pytest.fixture(scope="session")
def kit() -> Any:
    return _load_kit()

from dataclasses import replace
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import Discrete, WorldSpec
from rllab.methods import SpecMismatchError, Transition

@pytest.fixture
def learner(kit: Any) -> Any:
    return kit.double("methods.Learner", "dqn_snake_10")


@pytest.fixture
def snake(kit: Any) -> WorldSpec:
    spec: WorldSpec = kit.sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")
    return spec


def test_learner_acts_inside_the_action_space(kit: Any, learner: Any, snake: WorldSpec) -> None:
    assert isinstance(snake.action, Discrete)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    for t in kit.decode(
        list[Transition], kit.read("methods.Learner", "dqn_snake_10")["transitions"]
    ):
        actions = learner.act(t.obs, rng)
        assert actions.shape == (len(t.obs),)
        assert all(0 <= a < snake.action.n for a in actions)


def test_learner_policy_is_frozen_while_it_trains(kit: Any, learner: Any) -> None:
    transitions = kit.decode(
        list[Transition], kit.read("methods.Learner", "dqn_snake_10")["transitions"]
    )
    policy, rng = learner.policy(), np.random.default_rng(0)
    before = [policy.act(t.obs, rng).tolist() for t in transitions]
    for t in transitions:
        assert learner.observe(t) is None
    assert [policy.act(t.obs, rng).tolist() for t in transitions] == before
    assert all(isinstance(k, str) for k in learner.state_dict())

def test_transition_rows_are_the_team_agents(kit: Any, name: str) -> None:
    t: Transition = kit.sample("methods.Transition", name)
    rows = len(t.obs)
    assert t.next_obs.shape == t.obs.shape
    assert len(t.actions) == len(t.rewards) == len(t.active) == rows

"""baselines: random draws, the RulePolicy replay doubles, and the rule_for and baseline_means
fakes."""

from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import ActionSpace, BaselineMeans, Box, Discrete


def inside(space: ActionSpace, action: np.ndarray[Any, Any]) -> bool:
    if isinstance(space, Discrete):
        return action.shape == () and 0 <= int(action) < space.n
    in_range = (action >= space.low) & (action <= space.high)
    return action.shape == (space.dims,) and bool(np.all(in_range))


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", ["snake_5", "fish_5"])
def test_random_draws_are_inside_the_declared_action_format(kit: Any, name: str) -> None:
    draws = kit.read("baselines.RandomPolicy", name)
    space = kit.decode(ActionSpace, draws["action"])
    calls = [np.asarray(call) for call in draws["actions"]]
    assert len(calls) == 5
    assert all(inside(space, row) for call in calls for row in call)


RULES = ["snake_rule", "fish_no_ball"]


@pytest.fixture(params=RULES)
def rule(kit: Any, request: pytest.FixtureRequest) -> Any:
    return kit.double("baselines.RulePolicy", request.param)


def test_rule_description_is_one_sentence(rule: Any) -> None:
    assert rule.description.endswith(".")
    assert ". " not in rule.description


@pytest.mark.parametrize("name", RULES)
def test_rule_double_acts_as_recorded(kit: Any, name: str) -> None:
    rule = kit.double("baselines.RulePolicy", name)
    recording = kit.read("baselines.RulePolicy", name)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(0)
    for call in recording["calls"]:
        actions = rule.act(np.asarray(call["obs"], np.float32), rng)
        assert np.allclose(actions, np.asarray(call["actions"]))
        assert len(actions) == len(call["obs"])


def test_rule_for_gives_the_snake_rule(kit: Any) -> None:
    rule = kit.fake("baselines.rule_for")("snake", "default", "snake")
    assert rule.description.endswith(".")
    snake = kit.sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default")
    obs = np.asarray(kit.read("core.Policy", "snake_rule")["calls"][0]["obs"], np.float32)
    assert inside(snake.action, rule.act(obs, np.random.default_rng(0))[0])


@pytest.mark.parametrize(
    ("args", "named"),
    [(("chess", "default", "white"), "chess"), (("snake", "default", "fox"), "fox")],
)
def test_rule_for_names_what_has_no_rule(kit: Any, args: tuple[str, str, str], named: str) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match=named):
        kit.fake("baselines.rule_for")(*args)


def test_baseline_means_has_both_lines_for_every_team(kit: Any) -> None:
    means: BaselineMeans = kit.fake("baselines.baseline_means")("snake", "default")
    teams = {t.name for t in kit.sample("core.WorldSpec", "snake_default").teams}
    assert set(means.random) == set(means.rule) == teams
    assert (means.world, means.variant) == ("snake", "default")

    with pytest.raises(KeyError, match="chess"):
        kit.fake("baselines.baseline_means")("chess", "default")


def test_box_space_bounds_hold_for_the_fish_rule(kit: Any) -> None:
    rule = kit.double("baselines.RulePolicy", "fish_no_ball")
    call = kit.read("baselines.RulePolicy", "fish_no_ball")["calls"][0]
    pushes = rule.act(np.asarray(call["obs"], np.float32), np.random.default_rng(1))

from typing import Any

import numpy as np
import pytest

from rllab.core import Box, Discrete, Step, WorldSpec


@pytest.fixture
def make(kit: Any) -> Any:
    return kit.fake("worlds.make")


@pytest.fixture(params=["corridor", "target_point"])
def check_world(kit: Any, request: pytest.FixtureRequest) -> Any:
    return kit.double("worlds.CheckWorld", request.param)


def inside(spec: WorldSpec, action: np.ndarray[Any, Any]) -> bool:
    if isinstance(spec.action, Discrete):
        return action.shape == () and 0 <= int(action) < spec.action.n
    assert isinstance(spec.action, Box)
    in_range = (action >= spec.action.low) & (action <= spec.action.high)
    return action.shape == (spec.action.dims,) and bool(np.all(in_range))


def test_make_gives_a_world_whose_spec_reads_before_reset(make: Any) -> None:
    world = make("snake")
    assert world.spec.world == "snake"
    assert world.spec.variant == "default"
    assert world.spec.obs_len > 0

    ("args", "overrides", "named"),
    [
        (("chess",), {}, "chess"),
        (("snake", "huge"), {}, "huge"),
        (("snake",), {"lives": 3}, "lives"),
    ],
)
def test_make_names_an_unknown_world_variant_or_override(
    make: Any, args: tuple[str, ...], overrides: dict[str, int], named: str
) -> None:
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match=named):
        make(*args, **overrides)


def test_check_world_states_each_have_a_best_action_inside_the_space(check_world: Any) -> None:
    states, best = check_world.states(), check_world.best_actions()
    assert states.shape == (len(best), check_world.spec.obs_len)
    assert all(inside(check_world.spec, np.asarray(a)) for a in best)


def test_check_world_double_replays_its_recording(kit: Any, check_world: Any) -> None:
    name = "corridor" if check_world.spec.world == "check-discrete" else "target_point"
    recording = kit.read("worlds.CheckWorld", name)
    steps = kit.decode(list[Step], recording["steps"])
    check_world.reset(recording["seed"])
    for action, recorded in zip(recording["actions"], steps[1:], strict=True):
        assert np.array_equal(check_world.step(np.asarray(action)).obs, recorded.obs)
    assert steps[-1].done
